# Loaded avatar source catalog

Concise interoperability metadata from R7 global and R8 focused captures. All listed sources are VERIFIED_RUNTIME; suitability and untested playback remain separate in [avatar-candidates.md](avatar-candidates.md). R7 global capture was truncated; R8 NPC root was complete. This is not an exhaustive asset/prefab catalog. No mesh data, curves, textures or game source is included. Bone lists are the union of skin bindings, not proof of a Unity Humanoid Avatar.
